1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The homotopy variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 70 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 644 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 70 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homotopy structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 644. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
